14   implicit val resultApproachEnc = Injection[ResultApproach, String](
15     {
16       case CustomApproach(s) => s
17       case a => a.getClass.getSimpleName.dropRight(1)// drop the module $
18     },
19     s => s.toLowerCase match {
20       case "asis" => AsIs
21       case "mergefields" => MergeFields
22       case "staronly" => StarOnly
23       case "outputfieldonly" => OutputFieldOnly
24       case "outputfieldsonly" => OutputFieldsOnly
25       case _ => CustomApproach(s)
26     }
27   )
28 
29   implicit val runnerEnc = Injection[Runner, String](
30     {
31       case CustomRunnerEngine(s) => s
32       case a => a.getClass.getSimpleName.dropRight(1)// drop the module $
33     }, s =>
34       s.toLowerCase.replaceAll("_","") match {
35         case CollectRunnerName | "collectrunner" => Collect
36         case EngineRunnerName | "ruleengine" | "ruleenginerunner" => Engine
37         case FolderRunnerName | "fold" | "folderrunner" => Folder
38         case DQRunnerName | "dqrulerunner" | "rulerunner" => DQ
39         case _ => CustomRunnerEngine(s)
40       }
41   )
42

44     Injection[Duration, String]( _.toString , s => Duration(s) )
